A FORGENET file is\n// 8-byte magic, uint32 version 1, uint32 hidden size, then little-endian\n// int16 W1, B1, W2 and one int16 B2. A wrong magic, version or size leaves\n// the current net in place and writes the reason into error.\n[[nodiscard]] bool load_file(std::string_view path, std::string& error);\n\nvoid refresh(const Board& board, Accumulator& accumulator);\nvoid update(const Accumulator& parent, Accumulator& child, const DirtyPieces& dirty);\n// Side-to-move evaluation in centipawns.\n[[nodiscard]] int evaluate(const Board& board, const Accumulator& accumulator);\n\n// Piece changes implied by a move about to be played on `board`.\n[[nodiscard]] DirtyPieces dirty_pieces(const Board& board, const Move& move);\n\n} // namespace forge::nnue\n",

Ponder budgets start at ponderhit.\n    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point request_start{};\n    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point start_time{};\n};\n\nstruct SearchInfo {\n    Move best_move{};\n    Move ponder_move{};\n    int score = 0;\n    int depth = 0;\n    int seldepth = 0;\n    std::uint64_t nodes = 0;\n    std::uint64_t elapsed_ms = 0;\n    std::vector<Move> pv;\n    // A searchmoves intersection with no legal moves is not checkmate or stalemate.\n    bool restricted_root_empty = false;\n};\n\nconstexpr int MaxPly = 128;\nconstexpr int Infinite = 32001;\nconstexpr int MateValue = 32000;\nconstexpr int MateInMaxPly = MateValue - MaxPly;\nconstexpr int ValueNone = 32002;\n\n[[nodiscard]] constexpr bool is_mate_score(int score) {\n    return score >= MateInMaxPly || score <= -MateInMaxPly;\n}\n\nenum Bound : std::uint8_t { BoundNone = 0, BoundUpper = 1, BoundLower = 2, BoundExact = 3 };\n\nstruct TTEntry {\n    std::uint32_t key32 = 0;\n    Move move{};\n    std::int16_t score = 0;\n    std::int16_t eval = 0;\n    std::uint8_t depth = 0;\n    // Bits 0..1 bound, bit 2 was-PV, bits 3..7 generation.\n    std::uint8_t gen_bound = 0;\n    std::uint8_t rule50_clock = 0; // Capped at100; belongs to admitted score payload.\n    std::uint8_t padding = 0;\n\n    [[nodiscard]] Bound bound() const { return static_cast<Bound>(gen_bound & 3U); }\n    [[nodiscard]] bool pv() const { return (gen_bound & 4U) != 0; }\n    [[nodiscard]] std::uint8_t generation() const { return static_cast<std::uint8_t>(gen_bound >> 3U); }\n    [[nodiscard]] bool occupied() const { return bound() != BoundNone; }\n    // Existing below90 policy, plus exact near50 context; not a repetition key.\n    [[nodiscard]] bool score_usable(std::uint16_t current_clock) const {\n        const int capped = current_clock < 100 ? current_clock : 100;\n        return (rule50_clock < 90 && capped < 90) || rule50_clock == capped;\n    }\n};\n\nstruct alignas(64) TTCluster {\n    std::array<TTEntry, 4> entries{};\n};\nstatic_assert(sizeof(TTEntry) == 16);\nstatic_assert(sizeof(TTCluster) == 64);\n\nclass TranspositionTable;\n\n// Immutable entry copy plus an opaque probe-time replacement destination.\nstruct TTProbe {\n    TTEntry entry{};\n    bool hit = false;\nprivate:\n    friend class TranspositionTable;\n    const TranspositionTable* owner_ = nullptr;\n    std::size_t cluster_ = 0;\n    std::uint8_t slot_ = 0;\n    std::uint64_t epoch_ = 0;\n    std::uint64_t key_ = 0;\n};\n\n// Search reads and writes are coherent under striped locks. clear/resize/\n// new_search/destruction require all accessors quiescent (including diagnostic\n// readers and prefetch callers) and invalidate old probes. Weight columns may be only 16-byte\n// aligned, so those loads are unaligned; the accumulator and w2 stay aligned.\ntemplate <int NSub>\nint fuse_columns(const Accumulator& parent, Accumulator& child, const DirtyPieces& dirty, const Board& board,\n                  const Weights& w) {\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const float* const our_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    const float* const their_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    float* const our_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    float* const their_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    auto column = [&](Color perspective, const PieceChange& change) {\n        return w.w1.get() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature_index(perspective, change.piece, change.square)) * HiddenSize;\n    };\n    const float* const our_sub0 = column(us, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const their_sub0 = column(them, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const our_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(us, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const their_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(them, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const our_add = column(us, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const their_add = column(them, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const w2_ours = w.w2.data();\n    const float* const w2_theirs = w.w2.data() + HiddenSize;\n    const __m512 zero = _mm512_setzero_ps();\n    const __m512 one = _mm512_set1_ps(1.0F);\n    __m512 sum = zero;\n    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += 16) {\n        const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h);\n        __m512 ours = _mm512_sub_ps(_mm512_loadu_ps(our_src + base), _mm512_loadu_ps(our_sub0 + base));\n        __m512 theirs = _mm512_sub_ps(_mm512_loadu_ps(their_src + base), _mm512_loadu_ps(their_sub0 + base));\n        if constexpr (NSub == 2) {\n            ours = _mm512_sub_ps(ours, _mm512_loadu_ps(our_sub1 + base));\n            theirs = _mm512_sub_ps(theirs, _mm512_loadu_ps(their_sub1 + base));\n        }\n        ours = _mm512_add_ps(ours, _mm512_loadu_ps(our_add + base));\n        theirs = _mm512_add_ps(theirs, _mm512_loadu_ps(their_add + base));\n        _mm512_store_ps(our_dst + base, ours);\n        _mm512_store_ps(their_dst + base, theirs);\n        const __m512 a = _mm512_min_ps(_mm512_max_ps(ours, zero), one);\n        const __m512 b = _mm512_min_ps(_mm512_max_ps(theirs, zero), one);\n        const __m512 term = _mm512_add_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(a, a), _mm512_load_ps(w2_ours + base)),\n                                          _mm512_mul_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(b, b), _mm512_load_ps(w2_theirs + base)));\n        sum = _mm512_add_ps(sum, term);\n    }\n    alignas(64) float lanes[16];\n    _mm512_store_ps(lanes, sum);\n    child.valid = true;\n    return centipawns_from_lanes(lanes, w.b2);\n}\n#elif defined(__AVX2__)\n// One subtraction per removed feature, then one addition. The screlu sum uses the register just stored, in the same lane\n// order as evaluate().\ntemplate <int NSub>\nint fuse_columns(const Accumulator& parent, Accumulator& child, const DirtyPieces& dirty, const Board& board,\n                  const Weights& w) {\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const float* const our_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    const float* const their_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    float* const our_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    float* const their_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    auto column = [&](Color perspective, const PieceChange& change) {\n        return w.w1.get() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature_index(perspective, change.piece, change.square)) * HiddenSize;\n    };\n    const float* const our_sub0 = column(us, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const their_sub0 = column(them, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const our_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(us, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const their_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(them, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const our_add = column(us, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const their_add = column(them, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const w2_ours = w.w2.data();\n    const float* const w2_theirs = w.w2.data() + HiddenSize;\n    const __m256 zero = _mm256_setzero_ps();\n    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0F);\n    __m256 sums[2] = {zero, zero};\n    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += 16) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) {\n            const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h + 8 * k);\n            __m256 ours = _mm256_sub_ps(_mm256_load_ps(our_src + base), _mm256_load_ps(our_sub0 + base));\n            __m256 theirs = _mm256_sub_ps(_mm256_load_ps(their_src + base), _mm256_load_ps(their_sub0 + base));\n            if constexpr (NSub == 2) {\n                ours = _mm256_sub_ps(ours, _mm256_load_ps(our_sub1 + base));\n                theirs = _mm256_sub_ps(theirs, _mm256_load_ps(their_sub1 + base));\n            }\n            ours = _mm256_add_ps(ours, _mm256_load_ps(our_add + base));\n            theirs = _mm256_add_ps(theirs, _mm256_load_ps(their_add + base));\n            _mm256_store_ps(our_dst + base, ours);\n            _mm256_store_ps(their_dst + base, theirs);\n            const __m256 a = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(ours, zero), one);\n            const __m256 b = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(theirs, zero), one);\n            const __m256 term = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(a, a), _mm256_load_ps(w2_ours + base)),\n                                              _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(b, b), _mm256_load_ps(w2_theirs + base)));\n            sums[k] = _mm256_add_ps(sums[k], term);\n        }\n    }\n    alignas(64) float lanes[16];\n    for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) _mm256_store_ps(lanes + 8 * k, sums[k]);\n    child.valid = true;\n    return centipawns_from_lanes(lanes, w.b2);\n}\n#endif\n\n} // namespace\n\nint evaluate(const Board& board, const Accumulator& accumulator) {\n    const Weights& w = weights();\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const auto& ours = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)];\n    const auto& theirs = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(opposite(us))];\n    // Sixteen independent partial sums (lane j takes the hidden units with\n    // index % 16 == j, in increasing order) and then one ordered sum: the\n    // association is fixed by the source, so every target (SSE, AVX2, AVX-512,\n    // the scalar fallback) produces bit-identical results without -ffast-math.\n    constexpr int Lanes = 16;\n    static_assert(HiddenSize % Lanes == 0, \"HiddenSize must be a multiple of the lane count\");\n    alignas(64) float lanes[Lanes];\n#if FORGE_SPD_NNUE_ZMM\n    // One 512-bit register holds all sixteen lanes: lane j takes the hidden\n    // units h + j for h = 0, 16, 32, ... in increasing order, which is the\n    // lane order of the two 256-bit halves below (lane j of sums[j / 8]), and\n    // every term is formed with the same separate multiplies and additions in\n    // the same order, so each lane ends with the same bits as the AVX2 kernel.\n    {\n        const __m512 zero = _mm512_setzero_ps();\n        const __m512 one = _mm512_set1_ps(1.0F);\n        __m512 sum = zero;\n        for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; ++h) {\n                const std::size_t index = static_cast<std::size_t>(h);\n                destination[index] = ((source[index] - removed0_column[index]) - removed1_column[index]) + added_column[index];\n            }\n        }\n        child.valid = true;\n        return;\n    }\n    for (const Color perspective : {White, Black}) {\n        auto& acc = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(perspective)];\n        acc = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(perspective)];\n        for (int i = 0; i < dirty.count; ++i) {\n            const PieceChange& change = dirty.changes[static_cast<std::size_t>(i)];\n            const int index = feature_index(perspective, change.piece, change.square);\n            const float* column = w.w1.get() + static_cast<std::size_t>(index) * HiddenSize;\n            if (change.add) add_feature(acc, column);\n            else sub_feature(acc, column);\n        }\n    }\n    child.valid = true;\n}\n\nnamespace {\n\nint centipawns_from_lanes(const float lanes[16], float bias) {\n    float out = bias;\n    for (int j = 0; j < 16; ++j) out += lanes[j];\n    const float centipawns = out * 400.0F / SigmoidK;\n    return static_cast<int>(std::clamp(centipawns, -30000.0F, 30000.0F));\n}\n\n#if FORGE_SPD_NNUE_ZMM\n// Same feature arithmetic as update(), then the AVX-512 evaluate loop:\n// one 512-bit sum, lane j accumulating hidden units j, j+16, j+32, ...\n// with separate multiply and add. Weight columns may be only 16-byte\n// aligned, so those loads are unaligned; the accumulator and w2 stay aligned.\ntemplate <int NSub>\nint fuse_columns(const Accumulator& parent, Accumulator& child, const DirtyPieces& dirty, const Board& board,\n                  const Weights& w) {\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const float* const our_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    const float* const their_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    float* const our_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    float* const their_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    auto column = [&](Color perspective, const PieceChange& change) {\n        return w.w1.get() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature_index(perspective, change.piece, change.square)) * HiddenSize;\n    };\n    const float* const our_sub0 = column(us, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const their_sub0 = column(them, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const our_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(us, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const their_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(them, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const our_add = column(us, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const their_add = column(them, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const w2_ours = w.w2.data();\n    const float* const w2_theirs = w.w2.data() + HiddenSize;\n    const __m512 zero = _mm512_setzero_ps();\n    const __m512 one = _mm512_set1_ps(1.0F);\n    __m512 sum = zero;\n    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += 16) {\n        const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h);\n        __m512 ours = _mm512_sub_ps(_mm512_loadu_ps(our_src + base), _mm512_loadu_ps(our_sub0 + base));\n        __m512 theirs = _mm512_sub_ps(_mm512_loadu_ps(their_src + base), _mm512_loadu_ps(their_sub0 + base));\n        if constexpr (NSub == 2) {\n            ours = _mm512_sub_ps(ours, _mm512_loadu_ps(our_sub1 + base));\n            theirs = _mm512_sub_ps(theirs, _mm512_loadu_ps(their_sub1 + base));\n        }\n        ours = _mm512_add_ps(ours, _mm512_loadu_ps(our_add + base));\n        theirs = _mm512_add_ps(theirs, _mm512_loadu_ps(their_add + base));\n        _mm512_store_ps(our_dst + base, ours);\n        _mm512_store_ps(their_dst + base, theirs);\n        const __m512 a = _mm512_min_ps(_mm512_max_ps(ours, zero), one);\n        const __m512 b = _mm512_min_ps(_mm512_max_ps(theirs, zero), one);\n        const __m512 term = _mm512_add_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(a, a), _mm512_load_ps(w2_ours + base)),\n                                          _mm512_mul_ps(_mm512_mul_ps(b, b), _mm512_load_ps(w2_theirs + base)));\n        sum = _mm512_add_ps(sum, term);\n    }\n    alignas(64) float lanes[16];\n    _mm512_store_ps(lanes, sum);\n    child.valid = true;\n    return centipawns_from_lanes(lanes, w.b2);\n}\n#elif defined(__AVX2__)\n// One subtraction per removed feature, then one addition. NSub 1 is a quiet\n// move: (source - removed) + added. NSub 2 is a capture:\n// ((source - removed0) - removed1) + added. That is the same association as\n// update(). The screlu sum uses the register just stored, in the same lane\n// order as evaluate().\ntemplate <int NSub>\nint fuse_columns(const Accumulator& parent, Accumulator& child, const DirtyPieces& dirty, const Board& board,\n                  const Weights& w) {\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const Color them = opposite(us);\n    const float* const our_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    const float* const their_src = parent.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    float* const our_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)].data();\n    float* const their_dst = child.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(them)].data();\n    auto column = [&](Color perspective, const PieceChange& change) {\n        return w.w1.get() + static_cast<std::size_t>(feature_index(perspective, change.piece, change.square)) * HiddenSize;\n    };\n    const float* const our_sub0 = column(us, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const their_sub0 = column(them, dirty.changes[0]);\n    const float* const our_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(us, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const their_sub1 = NSub == 2 ? column(them, dirty.changes[1]) : nullptr;\n    const float* const our_add = column(us, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const their_add = column(them, dirty.changes[NSub]);\n    const float* const w2_ours = w.w2.data();\n    const float* const w2_theirs = w.w2.data() + HiddenSize;\n    const __m256 zero = _mm256_setzero_ps();\n    const __m256 one = _mm256_set1_ps(1.0F);\n    __m256 sums[2] = {zero, zero};\n    for (int h = 0; h < HiddenSize; h += 16) {\n        for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) {\n            const std::size_t base = static_cast<std::size_t>(h + 8 * k);\n            __m256 ours = _mm256_sub_ps(_mm256_load_ps(our_src + base), _mm256_load_ps(our_sub0 + base));\n            __m256 theirs = _mm256_sub_ps(_mm256_load_ps(their_src + base), _mm256_load_ps(their_sub0 + base));\n            if constexpr (NSub == 2) {\n                ours = _mm256_sub_ps(ours, _mm256_load_ps(our_sub1 + base));\n                theirs = _mm256_sub_ps(theirs, _mm256_load_ps(their_sub1 + base));\n            }\n            ours = _mm256_add_ps(ours, _mm256_load_ps(our_add + base));\n            theirs = _mm256_add_ps(theirs, _mm256_load_ps(their_add + base));\n            _mm256_store_ps(our_dst + base, ours);\n            _mm256_store_ps(their_dst + base, theirs);\n            const __m256 a = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(ours, zero), one);\n            const __m256 b = _mm256_min_ps(_mm256_max_ps(theirs, zero), one);\n            const __m256 term = _mm256_add_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(a, a), _mm256_load_ps(w2_ours + base)),\n                                              _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_mul_ps(b, b), _mm256_load_ps(w2_theirs + base)));\n            sums[k] = _mm256_add_ps(sums[k], term);\n        }\n    }\n    alignas(64) float lanes[16];\n    for (int k = 0; k < 2; ++k) _mm256_store_ps(lanes + 8 * k, sums[k]);\n    child.valid = true;\n    return centipawns_from_lanes(lanes, w.b2);\n}\n#endif\n\n} // namespace\n\nint evaluate(const Board& board, const Accumulator& accumulator) {\n    const Weights& w = weights();\n    const Color us = board.side_to_move();\n    const auto& ours = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(us)];\n    const auto& theirs = accumulator.values[static_cast<std::size_t>(opposite(us))];\n    // Sixteen independent partial sums (lane j takes the hidden units with\n    // index % 16 == j, in increasing order) and then one ordered sum: the\n    // association is fixed by the source, so every target (SSE, AVX2, AVX-512,\n    // the scalar fallback) produces bit-identical results without -ffast-math.\n    constexpr int Lanes = 16;\n    static_assert(HiddenSize % Lanes == 0, \"HiddenSize must be a multiple of the lane count\");\n    alignas(64) float lanes[Lanes];\n#if FORGE_SPD_NNUE_ZMM\n    // One 512-bit register holds all sixteen lanes: lane j takes the hidden\n    // units h + j for h = 0, 16, 32, ... in increasing order, which is the\n    // lane order of the two 256-bit halves below (lane j of sums[j / 8]), and\n    // every term is formed with the same separate multiplies and additions in\n    // the same order, so each lane ends with the same bits as the AVX2 kernel.\n    {\n        const __m512 zero = _mm512_setzero_ps();\n        const __m512 one = _mm512_set1_ps(1.0F);\n        __m512 sum = zero;\n        for (int h = 0; k < 2; ++k) _mm256_store_ps(lanes + 8 * k, sums[k]);\n#elif FORGE_NNUE_SSE\n    const __m128 zero = _mm_setzero_ps();\n    const __m128 one = _mm_set1_ps(1.0F);\n    __m128 sums[4] = {zero, zero, zero, zero};\n    for (int h = 0;
